function createOracleInterpreter(corpus) {
  "use strict";
  if (!corpus || typeof corpus !== "object") throw new TypeError("Oracle interpretation corpus is required.");
  const VERSION = "oracle-reading-2.3";
  const DOMAIN_KEYS = ["general", "work", "relationships", "decision", "selfcare"];
  const DOMAIN_DEFAULTS = {
    general: { label: "日常方向", intention: "把注意力放回此刻能看見、能實行的一件事。", question: "今天，我想把注意力放在哪裡？" },
    work: { label: "工作與創作", intention: "釐清成果、合作與資源，把想法轉成可檢查的下一步。", question: "在這件工作或創作中，我現在需要看見什麼？" },
    relationships: { label: "關係與溝通", intention: "辨認自己的需求與界線，以可觀察的互動代替猜測。", question: "這段關係裡，我現在能如何表達與回應？" },
    decision: { label: "選擇與決策", intention: "整理選項、條件與代價，先做可以回頭的小測試。", question: "面對這個選擇，我還需要確認什麼？" },
    selfcare: { label: "身心節奏", intention: "看見負荷、休息與日常支持，找出做得久的小調整。", question: "此刻，我需要怎樣照顧自己的節奏？" }
  };
  const hasOwn = (value, key) => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key);
  const deepFreeze = value => {
    if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Object.isFrozen(value)) return value;
    Object.keys(value).forEach(key => deepFreeze(value[key]));
    return Object.freeze(value);
  };
  const clean = value => typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  const firstSentence = value => {
    const text = clean(value);
    const sentence = text.match(/^[\s\S]*?[。！？.!?](?:[」』”’])?/);
    return sentence ? sentence[0] : text;
  };
  const nonempty = (value, where) => {
    const text = clean(value);
    if (!text) throw new TypeError("Missing authored oracle field: " + where);
    return text;
  };
  const toRecords = value => Array.isArray(value) ? value : Object.values(value || {});
  const profileRecords = toRecords(corpus.profiles);
  if (profileRecords.length !== 24) throw new RangeError("The Moonlit corpus must contain exactly 24 profiles.");
  const profiles = Object.create(null);
  const expectedIds = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, index) => "moonlit-" + String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0"));
  const profileFields = ["name", "group", "symbolism", "focus", "resource", "shadow", "present", "obstacle", "advice"];
  profileRecords.forEach(raw => {
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object" || !expectedIds.includes(raw.id) || hasOwn(profiles, raw.id)) {
      throw new TypeError("An oracle profile has an unknown or repeated card ID.");
    }
    const profile = { id: raw.id };
    profileFields.forEach(key => { profile[key] = nonempty(raw[key], raw.id + "." + key); });
    profile.domains = Object.create(null);
    DOMAIN_KEYS.forEach(key => {
      const source = raw.domains && raw.domains[key];
      if (!source || typeof source !== "object") throw new TypeError("Missing domain profile: " + raw.id + "." + key);
      profile.domains[key] = {
        reading: nonempty(source.reading, raw.id + "." + key + ".reading"),
        action: nonempty(source.action, raw.id + "." + key + ".action"),
        check: nonempty(source.check, raw.id + "." + key + ".check")
      };
    });
    profiles[raw.id] = deepFreeze(profile);
  });
  expectedIds.forEach(id => { if (!hasOwn(profiles, id)) throw new TypeError("Missing Moonlit card: " + id); });
  const providedDomains = toRecords(corpus.domains);
  const domains = DOMAIN_KEYS.map(id => {
    const source = (corpus.domains && corpus.domains[id]) || providedDomains.find(item => item && (item.id === id || item.key === id)) || {};
    return deepFreeze({
      id,
      label: clean(source.label) || DOMAIN_DEFAULTS[id].label,
      intention: clean(source.intention) || DOMAIN_DEFAULTS[id].intention,
      question: clean(source.question) || DOMAIN_DEFAULTS[id].question
    });
  });
  const domainMap = Object.fromEntries(domains.map(item => [item.id, item]));
  const pairs = Object.create(null);
  const pairSource = corpus.pairs || {};
  Object.keys(pairSource).forEach(key => {
    const split = key.split("|");
    if (split.length !== 2 || split[0] === split[1] || !hasOwn(profiles, split[0]) || !hasOwn(profiles, split[1])) {
      throw new TypeError("An ordered oracle pair has an invalid key: " + key);
    }
    const source = pairSource[key];
    if (!source || typeof source !== "object") throw new TypeError("Missing authored oracle pair: " + key);
    pairs[key] = deepFreeze({
      bridge: nonempty(source.bridge, key + ".bridge"),
      tension: nonempty(source.tension, key + ".tension"),
      transition: nonempty(source.transition, key + ".transition")
    });
  });
  const pairCount = Object.keys(pairs).length;
  const pairComplete = pairCount === 24 * 23 && expectedIds.every(from => expectedIds.every(to => from === to || hasOwn(pairs, from + "|" + to)));
  if (pairCount && !pairComplete) throw new RangeError("The directed-pair corpus must contain all 552 ordered pairs.");
  const undirectedDifferentiated = pairComplete ? expectedIds.reduce((count, from, index) => count + expectedIds.slice(index + 1).filter(to => JSON.stringify(pairs[from + "|" + to]) !== JSON.stringify(pairs[to + "|" + from])).length, 0) : 0;
  if (pairComplete && undirectedDifferentiated !== 276) throw new RangeError("Reversing every ordered pair must change its authored interpretation.");
  const coverage = deepFreeze({ profiles: 24, domains: 5, directedPairs: pairCount, pairsComplete: pairComplete, undirectedDifferentiated, positionedTriples: pairComplete ? 24 * 23 * 22 : 0 });
  const classify = question => {
    const text = clean(question).normalize("NFKC").toLowerCase();
    const scores = { work: 0, relationships: 0, decision: 0, selfcare: 0 };
    const signals = {
      work: [
        [/工作|職場|事業|求職|轉職|換工作|履歷|面試|升遷|薪資|薪水|主管|同事|客戶|專案|項目|deadline|career|job|workplace/g, 3],
        [/創作|寫作|作品|設計|創業|商業|生意|業績|成交|任務|會議|報告|交付|productivity|project|business|creative/g, 2]
      ],
      relationships: [
        [/伴侶|夫妻|婚姻|戀愛|感情|愛情|分手|復合|交往|喜歡的人|曖昧|親子|家人|家庭|朋友|友情|relationship|partner|marriage|friendship/g, 3],
        [/關係|溝通|相處|衝突|表達需求|吵架|告白|約會|界線|communication|conversation/g, 2]
      ],
      decision: [
        [/選擇|抉擇|決策|決定|兩難|選項|取捨|要不要|該不該|去不去|留下還是|choice|decision|decide|option/g, 2],
        [/比較|利弊|代價|風險|後悔|試驗|試做|compare|trade.?off/g, 1]
      ],
      selfcare: [
        [/睡眠|睡覺|失眠|休息|疲憊|疲倦|倦怠|焦慮|壓力|情緒|身體|健康|呼吸|身心|療癒|照顧自己|自我照顧|自我關懷|self.?care|burnout|sleep|anxiety|stress/g, 3],
        [/作息|節奏|放鬆|負荷|精神|飲食|散步|冥想|休養|rest|wellbeing/g, 2]
      ]
    };
    Object.keys(signals).forEach(key => {
      signals[key].forEach(([pattern, weight]) => { const matches = text.match(pattern); if (matches) scores[key] += Math.min(2, matches.length) * weight; });
    });
    const ranked = Object.keys(scores).sort((a, b) => scores[b] - scores[a]);
    const first = ranked[0], second = ranked[1];
    if (!scores[first]) return { domain: "general", reason: "先以日常方向展開；也可以自行選擇更貼近提問的角度。", scores };
    if (scores[first] < 2 || (scores[second] > 0 && scores[first] - scores[second] < 3)) {
      return { domain: "general", reason: "提問涉及不只一個面向，先以日常方向展開；請選擇你最想聚焦的角度。", scores };
    }
    return { domain: first, reason: "依提問先聚焦「" + domainMap[first].label + "」；你可以更換角度。", scores };
  };
  const detectDomain = question => classify(question).domain;
  const roleLabels = { single: "今日提醒", present: "現況", obstacle: "阻礙", advice: "建議" };
  const roleQuestions = {
    single: "這張牌如何回應我此刻的提問？",
    present: "目前有哪些資源、習慣或狀態值得看清？",
    obstacle: "這個主題失衡時，會在哪裡形成卡點？",
    advice: "下一步如何小而具體地改變我的回應？"
  };
  const fallbackPair = (from, to) => {
    const interactions = corpus.interactions || {};
    const authored = interactions[from.group + "|" + to.group] || interactions[from.group + "→" + to.group];
    if (authored && typeof authored === "object" && clean(authored.bridge) && clean(authored.tension) && clean(authored.transition)) {
      return { bridge: clean(authored.bridge), tension: clean(authored.tension), transition: clean(authored.transition), basis: "authored-group-draft" };
    }
    return {
      bridge: from.resource + " " + to.advice,
      tension: from.present + " " + to.shadow,
      transition: from.obstacle + " " + to.advice,
      basis: "profile-draft"
    };
  };
  const makePair = (from, to, key, label, component) => {
    const pair = pairs[from.id + "|" + to.id];
    const source = pair || fallbackPair(from, to);
    return {
      key,
      label,
      fromId: from.id,
      toId: to.id,
      fromName: from.name,
      toName: to.name,
      pairKey: from.id + "|" + to.id,
      bridge: source.bridge,
      tension: source.tension,
      transition: source.transition,
      reading: source[component],
      basis: pair ? "authored-directed-pair" : source.basis
    };
  };
  const REVIEW = {
    general: { action: "第7天把3次紀錄放在一起：選出真正幫你把注意力放回提問的一步；保留它，刪去只增加負擔的一步。", check: "能否指出一個實際做出的改變，以及支持這個判斷的紀錄？", question: "我看見了什麼實際改變，而不只是讀到一句喜歡的話？" },
    work: { action: "第7天對照3次紀錄和實際產出：哪個調整減少卡關或返工？留下有證據的一步，再決定是否擴大。", check: "比較前後的完成情況、返工或中斷；只採用你實際記錄過的指標。", question: "我交付了什麼、卡在哪一步，以及哪個小調整值得繼續？" },
    relationships: { action: "第7天回看3次互動：區分自己有說清的需求、對方實際的回應與尚未確認的猜測，再決定下一次怎麼說。", check: "是否留下具體說過的話與實際回應？界線是否更清楚、也能由自己維持？", question: "哪些是可見的互動，哪些仍需要直接問清楚？" },
    decision: { action: "第7天對照原本的條件和3次小測試：補齊缺少的資訊，保留有證據支持的選項，寫下一個可回頭的決定。", check: "是否能用實測紀錄說明選項的代價、可行性與仍未知的條件？", question: "什麼新證據會讓我改變選擇，而不只是尋找支持原決定的理由？" },
    selfcare: { action: "第7天比較3次調整前後的負荷與恢復感：保留容易持續的一步；若多做反而更累，就縮小份量或換方式。", check: "以自己的紀錄比較負荷、恢復感與可持續性；一次感受不代表長期結果。", question: "這個做法能融入我的日常，還是又變成一個必須完成的要求？" }
  };
  const interpret = (entry, optionalDomain) => {
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object" || !Array.isArray(entry.cardIds) || ![1, 3].includes(entry.cardIds.length)) {
      throw new TypeError("An oracle reading requires one or three card IDs.");
    }
    const ids = entry.cardIds.slice();
    if (new Set(ids).size !== ids.length || ids.some(id => !hasOwn(profiles, id))) {
      throw new TypeError("An oracle reading contains an unknown or repeated card ID.");
    }
    const question = clean(entry.question);
    const classified = classify(question);
    const requested = optionalDomain === undefined || optionalDomain === null || optionalDomain === "" ? entry.interpretationDomain : optionalDomain;
    const overridden = DOMAIN_KEYS.includes(requested);
    const domain = overridden ? requested : classified.domain;
    const domainInfo = domainMap[domain];
    const roleKeys = ids.length === 1 ? ["single"] : ["present", "obstacle", "advice"];
    const readingCards = ids.map((id, index) => {
      const profile = profiles[id], context = profile.domains[domain], roleKey = roleKeys[index];
      return {
        id, name: profile.name, group: profile.group, roleKey,
        roleLabel: roleLabels[roleKey], roleQuestion: roleQuestions[roleKey],
        symbolism: profile.symbolism, focus: profile.focus, resource: profile.resource, shadow: profile.shadow,
        roleReading: roleKey === "single" ? profile.present.replace(/^在現況位置，/, "此刻，") : profile[roleKey],
        contextReading: context.reading, action: context.action, check: context.check,
        balance: roleKey === "obstacle" ? profile.shadow : profile.resource
      };
    });
    const chosen = readingCards[readingCards.length - 1];
    const threePairs = ids.length === 3 ? [
      makePair(profiles[ids[0]], profiles[ids[1]], "present-obstacle", "現況 → 阻礙：資源如何變成卡點", "tension"),
      makePair(profiles[ids[1]], profiles[ids[2]], "obstacle-advice", "阻礙 → 建議：卡點如何轉成下一步", "transition"),
      makePair(profiles[ids[0]], profiles[ids[2]], "present-advice", "現況 → 建議：下一步承接哪些資源", "bridge")
    ] : [];
    const summary = ids.length === 1 ? chosen.contextReading : "現況｜" + readingCards[0].roleReading + "\n阻礙｜" + readingCards[1].roleReading + "\n轉向｜" + threePairs[1].transition + "\n承接｜" + threePairs[2].bridge;
    const contradiction = ids.length === 1 ? chosen.shadow : threePairs[0].tension;
    const resolution = ids.length === 1 ? profiles[ids[0]].advice : chosen.roleReading;
    const obstacleCue = ids.length === 1 ? chosen.shadow : readingCards[1].roleReading;
    const planRationale = ids.length === 1 ? chosen.roleReading : threePairs[1].transition;
    const overall = ids.length === 1 ? {
      state: chosen.roleReading, friction: chosen.shadow, route: chosen.action,
      continuity: chosen.resource, resolution, decisionCriterion: chosen.check
    } : {
      state: readingCards[0].roleReading, friction: threePairs[0].tension,
      route: threePairs[1].transition, continuity: threePairs[2].bridge,
      resolution, decisionCriterion: chosen.check
    };
    const plan = [
      { key: "today", label: "今天，先做一次", when: "今天 · 做出一個小步驟", action: chosen.action, check: chosen.check, rationale: planRationale, obstacleCue },
      { key: "experiment", label: "3天，做一個小實驗", when: "接下來3天 · 同一小步再試兩次", action: "沿用這個小步驟再試兩次：" + firstSentence(chosen.action) + "每次只改一項條件，並用同一指標記錄前後差異。", check: chosen.check, rationale: planRationale, obstacleCue },
      { key: "review", label: "7天，依紀錄調整", when: "第7天 · 回看3次紀錄", action: REVIEW[domain].action, check: REVIEW[domain].check, rationale: ids.length === 1 ? chosen.resource : threePairs[2].bridge, obstacleCue }
    ];
    return deepFreeze({
      version: VERSION,
      corpusVersion: clean(corpus.version) || clean(corpus.schema) || "moonlit-authored-depth-v1",
      pairVersion: clean(corpus.pairVersion) || "moonlit-directed-pairs-v1",
      domain, domainLabel: domainInfo.label, domainIntention: domainInfo.intention,
      domainReason: overridden ? "你選擇以「" + domainInfo.label + "」展開這次提問。" : classified.reason,
      domainSource: overridden ? "selected" : "question",
      question, mode: ids.length === 1 ? "single" : "three",
      cardIds: ids, cards: readingCards,
      headline: ids.length === 1 ? chosen.name + "｜讓一個小步驟回應你的提問" : "從現況看見卡點，讓建議落在可做的一步",
      summary, overview: ids.length === 1 ? firstSentence(summary) : threePairs[1].transition, overall,
      connection: ids.length === 1 ? chosen.resource : threePairs[2].bridge,
      contradiction, resolution, threePairs, plan,
      action: chosen.action, check: chosen.check,
      reviewQuestions: [chosen.roleQuestion, REVIEW[domain].question],
      basis: {
        kind: "authored-original-oracle",
        label: "月光手札原創牌義 · 依提問角度與牌位展開",
        method: ids.length === 1 ? "以原創圖像、資源與失衡兩面、提問角度及可觀察行動展開。" : "以原創牌義、現況／阻礙／建議牌位與3組有方向的兩牌關係組合解讀。",
        profiles: ids.slice(), pairs: threePairs.map(pair => pair.pairKey),
        completeDirectedPairs: pairComplete,
        generatedPositionedTriple: ids.length === 3,
        assurance: "行動計畫是可調整的探索建議；觀察紀錄後再決定是否繼續。"
      }
    });
  };
  return deepFreeze({ version: VERSION, profiles, domains, pairs, coverage, interpret, detectDomain });
}

const oracleInterpreter=createOracleInterpreter(oracleReadingCorpus);
Object.defineProperty(window,"oracleReading",{value:oracleInterpreter,writable:false,configurable:false});


/* ORACLE_READING_DEPTH_UI_BEGIN */
let readingSection="overview",readingDetailSection="position",readingEdge=0,readingRelationLens="role",readingPlanStep=0,readingPlanLens="steps",readingAnalysis=null;
const readingSections=[["overview","總覽"],["detail","牌義"],["relations","關係"],["plan","行動"]];
const domainShort={general:"方向",work:"工作",relationships:"關係",decision:"選擇",selfcare:"照顧"};
function readingNode(tag,className,text){
 const n=document.createElement(tag);if(className)n.className=className;if(text!==undefined)n.textContent=text;return n;
}
function readingParagraph(parent,label,text,className=""){
 const section=readingNode("section","reading-detail-block "+className);
 if(label)section.append(readingNode("h5","",label));
 section.append(readingNode("p","",text));parent.append(section);return section;
}
function readingToggleButton(id,label,onClick){
 const button=readingNode("button","",label);button.type="button";button.id=id;button.addEventListener("click",onClick);return button;
}
function ensureReadingDepth(){
 if(byId("reading-toolbar"))return;
 const grid=byId("result-grid"),toolbar=readingNode("div","reading-toolbar");toolbar.id="reading-toolbar";
 const tabs=readingNode("div","reading-tabs");tabs.setAttribute("role","tablist");tabs.setAttribute("aria-label","同頁閱讀解讀");
 for(const [key,label]of readingSections){
  const b=readingToggleButton("reading-tab-"+key,label,()=>setReadingSection(key));
  b.dataset.readingSection=key;b.setAttribute("role","tab");b.setAttribute("aria-controls",key==="overview"?"reading-overview":"reading-workspace");tabs.append(b);
 }
 tabs.addEventListener("keydown",event=>{
  if(!["ArrowLeft","ArrowRight","Home","End"].includes(event.key))return;
  const index=readingSections.findIndex(([key])=>key===readingSection);
  const next=event.key==="Home"?0:event.key==="End"?readingSections.length-1:(index+(event.key==="ArrowRight"?1:-1)+readingSections.length)%readingSections.length;
  event.preventDefault();setReadingSection(readingSections[next][0]);byId("reading-tab-"+readingSections[next][0]).focus({preventScroll:true});
 });
 const label=readingNode("label","reading-domain-label");label.htmlFor="reading-domain";label.append(readingNode("span","visually-hidden","解讀角度"));
 const select=readingNode("select","");select.id="reading-domain";select.setAttribute("aria-label","解讀角度；自動依提問判斷，也可以自行選擇");
 for(const [id,text]of [["auto","自動"],["general","方向"],["work","工作"],["relationships","關係"],["decision","選擇"],["selfcare","照顧"]]){
  const option=readingNode("option","",text);option.value=id;select.append(option);
 }
 select.addEventListener("change",()=>{
  const entry=currentEntry();if(!entry)return;flushNote();const note=byId("journal").value.slice(0,4000);
  entry.note=note;if(select.value==="auto")delete entry.interpretationDomain;else entry.interpretationDomain=select.value;
  touch(entry);persist();rebuildReadingDepth(entry);syncReadingPanels();
  announce("save-status",storageReady?"解讀角度與心得已儲存於目前的瀏覽器。":"解讀角度與心得保留在本次頁面；請下載備份。");
 });
 label.append(select);toolbar.append(tabs,label);
 const overview=readingNode("section","reading-overview");overview.id="reading-overview";overview.setAttribute("role","tabpanel");overview.setAttribute("aria-labelledby","reading-tab-overview");
 const synthesis=readingNode("p","reading-synthesis");synthesis.id="reading-synthesis";overview.append(synthesis);
 const workspace=readingNode("section","reading-workspace");workspace.id="reading-workspace";workspace.hidden=true;workspace.setAttribute("role","tabpanel");
 grid.before(toolbar,overview);grid.after(workspace);
}
function setReadingSection(key){
 if(!readingSections.some(([k])=>k===key))return;readingSection=key;syncReadingDepthVisibility();syncReadingPanels();
}
function rebuildReadingDepth(entry){
 ensureReadingDepth();readingAnalysis=oracleInterpreter.interpret(entry,entry.interpretationDomain);
 byId("reading-domain").value=entry.interpretationDomain||"auto";
 byId("reading-domain").querySelector('option[value="auto"]').textContent="自動："+domainShort[readingAnalysis.domain];
 if(entry.cardIds.length===1)byId("result-title").textContent=cardMap.get(entry.cardIds[0]).name+"｜"+readingAnalysis.domainLabel+"的提醒";
 byId("reading-synthesis").textContent=readingAnalysis.overview;
 const workspace=byId("reading-workspace");workspace.replaceChildren();
 const detail=readingNode("div","reading-detail");detail.id="reading-detail";detail.hidden=true;
 const detailTabs=readingNode("div","reading-subtabs");detailTabs.setAttribute("role","group");detailTabs.setAttribute("aria-label","牌義的三個層次");
 for(const [key,label]of [["symbol","牌面線索"],["position","提問與牌位"],["balance","亮面與盲點"]]){
  const b=readingToggleButton("reading-detail-"+key,label,()=>{readingDetailSection=key;syncReadingDepthVisibility();});
  b.dataset.readingDetail=key;detailTabs.append(b);
 }
 const detailGrid=readingNode("div","reading-detail-grid");detailGrid.id="reading-detail-grid";
 readingAnalysis.cards.forEach((c,pos)=>{
  const article=readingNode("article","reading-deep-card");article.id="reading-deep-panel-"+pos;article.setAttribute("aria-labelledby","reading-deep-heading-"+pos);
  const heading=readingNode("h4","",c.roleLabel+"｜"+c.name);heading.id="reading-deep-heading-"+pos;article.append(heading);
  for(const key of ["symbol","position","balance"]){
   const pane=readingNode("div","reading-detail-pane");pane.dataset.detailPane=key;
   if(key==="symbol"){readingParagraph(pane,"從牌面看見",c.symbolism);readingParagraph(pane,"這張牌的核心",c.focus);}
   if(key==="position"){readingParagraph(pane,"這個位置的含義",c.roleReading);readingParagraph(pane,readingAnalysis.domainLabel+"中的應用",c.contextReading);}
   if(key==="balance"){readingParagraph(pane,"可以運用的力量",c.resource);readingParagraph(pane,"需要核對的盲點",c.shadow);}
   article.append(pane);
  }
  detailGrid.append(article);
 });
 detail.append(detailTabs,detailGrid);workspace.append(detail);
 const relation=readingNode("div","reading-relations");relation.id="reading-relations";relation.hidden=true;
 const relationIntro=readingNode("div","reading-relation-top");relationIntro.append(readingNode("p","","把三張牌放回同一條思路。"));
 const catalog=readingNode("a","reading-catalog-link","查閱全部 12,144 組");catalog.href="/oracle-relations.html";catalog.target="_blank";catalog.rel="noopener";relationIntro.append(catalog);relation.append(relationIntro);
 const edges=readingNode("div","reading-subtabs reading-edge-tabs");edges.setAttribute("role","group");edges.setAttribute("aria-label","三張牌之間的三條關係");
 const edgeGrid=readingNode("div","reading-detail-grid");edgeGrid.id="reading-edge-grid";
 (readingAnalysis.threePairs||[]).forEach((p,index)=>{
  const b=readingToggleButton("reading-edge-"+index,["現況 → 阻礙","阻礙 → 建議","現況 → 建議"][index],()=>{readingEdge=index;syncReadingDepthVisibility();});b.dataset.readingEdge=String(index);edges.append(b);
  const article=readingNode("article","reading-relation-card");article.id="reading-edge-panel-"+index;article.append(readingNode("h4","",["現況 → 阻礙","阻礙 → 建議","現況 → 建議"][index]+"｜"+cardMap.get(p.fromId).name+" → "+cardMap.get(p.toId).name));
  for(const [key,label,text]of [["role",["資源怎麼成為卡點","卡點如何找到出口","下一步承接的資源"][index],p.reading],["bridge","彼此如何呼應",p.bridge],["tension","容易拉扯的地方",p.tension],["transition","把關係轉成下一步",p.transition]]){
   const block=readingParagraph(article,label,text);block.dataset.relationLens=key;
  }
  edgeGrid.append(article);
 });
 if(!readingAnalysis.threePairs?.length){
  const article=readingNode("article","reading-relation-card");
  readingParagraph(article,"這次是一張牌","三牌關係需要現況、阻礙與建議三個位置。你可以先讀懂這張牌的力量與盲點，或到關係資料庫比較任何一組排列。");
  edgeGrid.append(article);
 }
 const relationTabs=readingNode("div","reading-subtabs");relationTabs.setAttribute("role","group");relationTabs.setAttribute("aria-label","這條關係的解讀層次");
 for(const [key,label]of [["role","這個牌位"],["bridge","呼應"],["tension","張力"],["transition","轉向"]]){
  const b=readingToggleButton("reading-relation-"+key,label,()=>{readingRelationLens=key;syncReadingDepthVisibility();});b.dataset.readingRelationLens=key;relationTabs.append(b);
 }relationTabs.hidden=!readingAnalysis.threePairs.length;
 relation.append(edges,relationTabs,edgeGrid);workspace.append(relation);
 const plan=readingNode("div","reading-plan");plan.id="reading-plan";plan.hidden=true;
 const planTabs=readingNode("div","reading-subtabs reading-plan-tabs");planTabs.setAttribute("role","group");planTabs.setAttribute("aria-label","依時間閱讀行動計畫");
 const planGrid=readingNode("div","reading-detail-grid");planGrid.id="reading-plan-grid";
 readingAnalysis.plan.forEach((p,index)=>{
  const b=readingToggleButton("reading-plan-"+index,["今天","3天實驗","7天回顧"][index],()=>{readingPlanStep=index;syncReadingDepthVisibility();});b.dataset.readingPlan=String(index);planTabs.append(b);
  const article=readingNode("article","reading-plan-card");article.id="reading-plan-panel-"+index;article.append(readingNode("h4","",p.when+"｜"+p.label));
  const steps=readingNode("div","");steps.dataset.planLens="steps";readingParagraph(steps,"具體做法",p.action);readingParagraph(steps,"到時核對",p.check);
  const reasons=readingNode("div","");reasons.dataset.planLens="why";readingParagraph(reasons,"為什麼從這裡開始",p.rationale);readingParagraph(reasons,"實行時留意的卡點",p.obstacleCue);
  article.append(steps,reasons);
  planGrid.append(article);
 });
 const planLenses=readingNode("div","reading-subtabs");planLenses.setAttribute("role","group");planLenses.setAttribute("aria-label","行動的做法與理由");
 for(const [key,label]of [["steps","做法與核對"],["why","背後的理由"]]){
  const b=readingToggleButton("reading-plan-lens-"+key,label,()=>{readingPlanLens=key;syncReadingDepthVisibility();});b.dataset.readingPlanLens=key;planLenses.append(b);
 }plan.append(planTabs,planLenses,planGrid);workspace.append(plan);
 syncReadingDepthVisibility();
}
function syncReadingDepthVisibility(){
 if(!byId("reading-toolbar"))return;
 const overview=readingSection==="overview";
 byId("reading-overview").hidden=!overview;byId("result-grid").hidden=!overview;byId("reading-workspace").hidden=overview;
 byId("reading-workspace").setAttribute("aria-labelledby","reading-tab-"+readingSection);
 byId("reading-toolbar").querySelectorAll("[data-reading-section]").forEach(b=>{const selected=b.dataset.readingSection===readingSection;b.setAttribute("aria-selected",String(selected));b.tabIndex=selected?0:-1;});
 for(const key of ["detail","relations","plan"])byId("reading-"+key).hidden=readingSection!==key;
 byId("reading-detail").querySelectorAll("[data-reading-detail]").forEach(b=>b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(b.dataset.readingDetail===readingDetailSection)));
 byId("reading-detail").querySelectorAll("[data-detail-pane]").forEach(p=>p.hidden=p.dataset.detailPane!==readingDetailSection);
 const narrow=readingNarrow.matches;
 byId("reading-relations").querySelectorAll("[data-reading-relation-lens]").forEach(b=>b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(b.dataset.readingRelationLens===readingRelationLens)));
 byId("reading-relations").querySelectorAll("[data-relation-lens]").forEach(p=>p.hidden=p.dataset.relationLens!==readingRelationLens);
 byId("reading-plan").querySelectorAll("[data-reading-plan-lens]").forEach(b=>b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(b.dataset.readingPlanLens===readingPlanLens)));
 byId("reading-plan").querySelectorAll("[data-plan-lens]").forEach(p=>p.hidden=p.dataset.planLens!==readingPlanLens);
 byId("reading-edge-grid").querySelectorAll(".reading-relation-card").forEach((p,i)=>p.hidden=narrow&&i!==readingEdge);
 byId("reading-relations").querySelectorAll("[data-reading-edge]").forEach(b=>{b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(Number(b.dataset.readingEdge)===readingEdge));b.setAttribute("aria-controls","reading-edge-panel-"+b.dataset.readingEdge);});
 byId("reading-plan-grid").querySelectorAll(".reading-plan-card").forEach((p,i)=>p.hidden=narrow&&i!==readingPlanStep);
 byId("reading-plan").querySelectorAll("[data-reading-plan]").forEach(b=>{b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(Number(b.dataset.readingPlan)===readingPlanStep));b.setAttribute("aria-controls","reading-plan-panel-"+b.dataset.readingPlan);});
 byId("reading-detail-grid").querySelectorAll(".reading-deep-card").forEach((p,i)=>p.hidden=narrow&&readingAnalysis.cards.length>1&&i!==readingSelected);
}
function appendDepthReadingText(entry,text){
 const r=oracleInterpreter.interpret(entry,entry.interpretationDomain);
 text+="\n深入解讀｜"+r.domainLabel+" · "+r.version+"\n"+r.summary+"\n\n";
 r.cards.forEach(c=>{
  text+=c.roleLabel+"｜"+c.name+"\n牌面線索："+c.symbolism+"\n核心："+c.focus+"\n牌位："+c.roleReading+"\n回應提問："+c.contextReading+"\n可用力量："+c.resource+"\n盲點："+c.shadow+"\n具體行動："+c.action+"\n核對："+c.check+"\n\n";
 });
 if(r.threePairs?.length){text+="三牌關係\n";r.threePairs.forEach(p=>{text+=p.label+"｜"+cardMap.get(p.fromId).name+" → "+cardMap.get(p.toId).name+"\n呼應："+p.bridge+"\n張力："+p.tension+"\n轉向："+p.transition+"\n\n";});}
 text+="行動計畫\n";r.plan.forEach(p=>text+=p.when+"｜"+p.label+"\n"+p.action+"\n核對："+p.check+"\n理由："+p.rationale+"\n留意："+p.obstacleCue+"\n\n");
 return text+"編寫依據："+r.basis.label+"；"+r.basis.method+"\n"+r.basis.assurance+"\n";
}
/* ORACLE_READING_DEPTH_UI_END */

function readingText(entry){
 const roles=entryRoles(entry),prompts=roleQuestions(entry);
 let text="神諭之間｜月光手札\n"+taipeiDay(entry.time)+" "+formatTime(entry.time)+"（臺北時間）\n\n你的提問："+entry.question+"\n\n";
 entry.cardIds.forEach((id,pos)=>{
  const c=cardMap.get(id);
  text+=roles[pos]+"｜"+c.name+"\n"+(prompts[pos]?prompts[pos]+"\n":"")+c.keywords+"\n"+c.message+"\n\n一個小行動："+c.action+"\n留給自己的問題："+c.prompt+"\n\n";
 });
 return appendDepthReadingText(entry,text+"我的第一眼感受：\n"+(entry.note||"（留白）")+"\n\n月光手札 · 24 張原創神諭卡 · 供自我探索與行動提醒。\n");
}

case "waves":shape=`<circle cx="90" cy="97" r="28" fill="#ccb989" opacity=".8"/><path d="M28 153Q55 115 85 151T153 149V225H28Z" fill="#234b5c"/><path d="M28 165Q58 140 91 167T153 164V226H28Z" fill="#376879"/><path d="M27 183Q57 157 88 184T153 179V225H27Z" fill="#61909a" opacity=".6"/><g stroke="#c5c4a4" stroke-width=".8" fill="none"><path d="M28 152Q55 115 85 151T153 149M28 165Q58 140 91 167T153 164M27 183Q57 157 88 184T153 179M42 197Q70 190 103 201T147 196"/></g>`;break;
case "roots":shape=`<circle cx="90" cy="125" r="54" fill="none" stroke="#b6b894" stroke-width=".6" opacity=".5"/><circle cx="90" cy="83" r="16" fill="#d6c698" opacity=".7"/><path d="M89 179Q83 146 91 106M90 130Q62 122 54 101Q80 98 90 130M87 147Q111 140 128 111Q96 112 87 147M87 158Q62 151 53 135Q79 132 87 158M88 176Q112 167 125 148Q100 146 88 176" fill="#687c65" stroke="#d3c79d" stroke-width=".8"/><g fill="none" stroke="#c8bd91" stroke-width=".85"><path d="M35 179H144M88 178Q89 197 73 216M88 183Q109 197 117 216M88 195L95 225M79 207L59 213M104 201L127 200M87 188L68 196"/></g>`;break;

case "mountains":shape=`<circle cx="91" cy="126" r="54" fill="none" stroke="#d0b787" stroke-width=".7"/><circle cx="91" cy="107" r="22" fill="#d8b28c" opacity=".7"/><path d="M30 199L69 125L101 183L122 154L151 199Z" fill="#51425c" stroke="#bca68f" stroke-width=".8"/><path d="M30 207L82 150L109 196L137 176L151 207Z" fill="#2c2537" stroke="#c9b892" stroke-width=".8"/><path d="M34 217H147" stroke="#bca68f" stroke-width=".7"/>`;break;
case "light":shape=`<circle cx="90" cy="139" r="56" fill="url(#glow-${uid})"/><g fill="none" stroke="#d8be8d" stroke-width=".7" opacity=".6"><circle cx="90" cy="139" r="48"/><circle cx="90" cy="139" r="57"/><path d="M90 69V55M90 222V208M20 139H7M173 139H160M39 89L30 79M143 189L151 200M39 189L30 199M142 89L151 79"/></g><path d="M83 146Q64 126 90 100Q110 127 96 146Z" fill="#dbbd87"/><path d="M87 145Q80 135 91 119Q100 136 93 145Z" fill="#f5e9c7"/><path d="M81 151H99V204H81Z" fill="#9c82a5" stroke="#d8be8d" stroke-width=".8"/><path d="M70 204H110M65 212H115" stroke="#d8be8d" stroke-width=".8"/>`;break;

}
let stars="";[[37,59,.6],[143,73,.7],[54,88,.4],[129,113,.45],[39,106,.35],[146,129,.4],[68,53,.35],[114,62,.45]].forEach(s=>stars+=star(...s));
return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 180 300" role="img" aria-label="${c.name}：${c.keywords}"><defs><linearGradient id="bg-${uid}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop stop-color="${c.color}"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#201927"/></linearGradient><radialGradient id="glow-${uid}"><stop stop-color="#dcc69e" stop-opacity=".34"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#b58ad1" stop-opacity="0"/></radialGradient></defs><rect width="180" height="300" fill="url(#bg-${uid})"/><rect x="9" y="9" width="162" height="282" rx="3" fill="none" stroke="#cbb284" stroke-width=".7"/><path d="M14 37V19H32M148 19H166V37M14 263V281H32M148 281H166V263" fill="none" stroke="#cbb284" stroke-width=".6"/><path d="M27 232V104a63 63 0 0 1 126 0V232" fill="none" stroke="#cbb284" stroke-width=".6" opacity=".5"/>${stars}${shape}${c.number>8?variation(c):""}<path d="M45 237H135" stroke="#cbb284" stroke-width=".5"/><text x="90" y="260" text-anchor="middle" fill="#eddfc0" font-family="serif" font-size="17" letter-spacing="5">${c.name}</text><text x="90" y="276" text-anchor="middle" fill="#ccb284" font-family="Georgia,serif" font-size="6.3" letter-spacing="1.8">${c.en}</text></svg>`;
}

function storageMessage(){
 if(storageReason==="other-tab")return "另一個分頁正在保存手札。這個分頁使用暫存，重新整理後可能遺失；請匯出備份，或關閉另一個分頁後重新載入。";
 if(storageReason==="unsupported")return "目前瀏覽器無法安全協調手札保存，這次使用暫存。重新整理後可能遺失；請匯出備份。";
 if(storageReason==="paused")return "已回到這次的手札。此分頁目前使用暫存；重新整理可重新確認保存方式。請先匯出新的感受。";
 return storageReady?"手札保留於目前的瀏覽器；不同裝置不會同步。可以匯出備份，再於其他瀏覽器匯入。":storageReason==="invalid"?"既有手札資料無法讀取，原資料已保留。這次使用頁面暫存，重新整理後可能遺失；請匯出備份。":"目前環境無法保留瀏覽器資料，這次使用頁面暫存。重新整理後可能遺失；請匯出備份。";
}
function loadStorage(writable=true){
 try{
  const raw=window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
  const parsed=parseState(raw);
  if(parsed.status==="invalid"){storageReason="invalid";return;}
  if(parsed.state)state=parsed.state;
  if(!writable)return;
  const probe=STORAGE_KEY+":probe";
  window.localStorage.setItem(probe,"1");
  window.localStorage.removeItem(probe);
  storageReady=true;
 }catch(error){storageReason="unavailable";}
}
function persist(){
 if(!storageReady){announce("storage-note",storageMessage());return false;}
 try{
  const raw=JSON.stringify(state);
  if(parseState(raw).status!=="ok")throw new Error("Invalid state");
  window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY,raw);
  return true;
 }catch(error){
  storageReady=false;storageReason="unavailable";
  announce("storage-note",storageMessage());
  return false;
 }
}
function flushNote(){
 if(noteTimer!==null){window.clearTimeout(noteTimer);noteTimer=null;const saved=persist();announce("save-status",saved?"感受已自動保存。":"心得已留在本次頁面，重新整理可能遺失；請匯出備份。");}
}
function touch(entry){
 entry.updatedAt=new Date(Math.max(Date.now(),Date.parse(entry.updatedAt))).toISOString();
}
function saveNote(show=true){
 const entry=currentEntry();
 if(!entry)return;
 flushNote();
 entry.note=byId("journal").value.slice(0,4000);
 touch(entry);
 const saved=persist();
 if(show)announce("save-status",saved?"已儲存於目前的瀏覽器。":"已保留於本次頁面，重新整理可能遺失；請匯出備份。");
 renderHistory();
}
function setBusy(value){
 busy=value;
 document.querySelectorAll("#draw-form button,#draw-form textarea").forEach(el=>el.disabled=value);
 byId("daily-button").disabled=value;
 byId("draw-again").disabled=value;
 byId("reshuffle-button").disabled=value;
 byId("ritual-deck").classList.toggle("shuffling",value);
 byId("draw-button").textContent=value?"正在洗牌，留一口呼吸……":mode===1?"洗牌，選一張牌 ✧":"洗牌，選三張牌 ✧";
}
function updateMode(value){
 if(busy)return;
 mode=value;
 document.querySelectorAll("[data-mode]").forEach(el=>el.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(Number(el.dataset.mode)===value)));
 selectedSlots=[];selectionDeck=[];selectionPhase="idle";
 byId("choice-panel").hidden=true;
 byId("draw-button").textContent=value===1?"洗牌，選一張牌 ✧":"洗牌，選三張牌 ✧";
 announce("draw-status",value===1?"已選擇一張牌，洗牌後挑選一張。":"已選擇三張牌，依序代表現況／阻礙／建議。");
}
function capacityAvailable(id){
 if(state.entries.length<MAX_ENTRIES||state.entries.some(e=>e.id===id))return true;
 announce("draw-status","手札已達 "+MAX_ENTRIES+" 份。請先匯出並刪除部分紀錄，再留下新的手札。");
 return false;
}
function beginSelection(){
 if(busy)return;
 flushNote();
 if(!capacityAvailable(null))return;
 setBusy(true);
 selectedSlots=[];selectionPhase="shuffling";
 announce("draw-status","正在洗牌，讓注意力慢慢回到自己。");
 window.setTimeout(()=>{
  selectionDeck=shuffledIds().slice(0,12);
  selectionPhase="selecting";
  const host=byId("choice-cards");
  host.replaceChildren();
  selectionDeck.forEach((id,index)=>{
   const button=document.createElement("button");
   button.type="button";button.className="pick-card";
   button.dataset.slot=String(index);
   button.setAttribute("aria-label","選擇第 "+(index+1)+" 張牌");
   button.setAttribute("aria-pressed","false");
   button.innerHTML='<span class="moon" aria-hidden="true">☾</span><span class="slot" aria-hidden="true">'+String(index+1).padStart(2,"0")+'</span><span class="order" aria-hidden="true" hidden></span>';
   button.addEventListener("click",()=>toggleSlot(index));
   host.append(button);
  });
  byId("choice-title").textContent=mode===1?"讓目光停留，選擇一張牌。":"循著直覺，依序選擇三張牌。";
  byId("choice-hint").textContent=mode===1?"已從 24 張牌洗出 12 張。先選一張牌背，再揭開提醒。":"已從 24 張牌洗出 12 張。選取順序：現況 → 阻礙 → 建議。";
  byId("choice-panel").hidden=false;
  updateSelection();
  setBusy(false);
  announce("draw-status","洗牌完成。請從牌背選取 "+mode+" 張牌。");
  byId("choice-title").setAttribute("tabindex","-1");
/* ORACLE_SOURCE_SUCCESS_HOOK_draw_BEGIN */
if(selectionPhase==="selecting"&&!byId("choice-panel").hidden){
 if(window.oracleUI)window.oracleUI.show("draw",{route:false,focus:false});
 const firstChoice=byId("choice-cards").querySelector(".pick-card");
 if(!window.oracleViewportPages?.showNode?.(firstChoice,{focus:true})){
  byId("choice-title").focus({preventScroll:true});
  byId("choice-panel").scrollIntoView({behavior:reduceMotion()?"auto":"smooth",block:"start"});
 }
}
/* ORACLE_SOURCE_SUCCESS_HOOK_draw_END */
 },reduceMotion()?50:650);
}
function toggleSlot(index){
 if(selectionPhase!=="selecting")return;
 const at=selectedSlots.indexOf(index);
 if(at>=0)selectedSlots.splice(at,1);
 else if(selectedSlots.length<mode)selectedSlots.push(index);
 updateSelection();
}
function updateSelection(){
 document.querySelectorAll("[data-slot]").forEach(button=>{
  const order=selectedSlots.indexOf(Number(button.dataset.slot));
  button.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(order>=0));
  button.disabled=order<0&&selectedSlots.length===mode;
  const marker=button.querySelector(".order");
  marker.hidden=order<0;
  marker.textContent=order>=0?String(order+1):"";
  button.setAttribute("aria-label","第 "+(Number(button.dataset.slot)+1)+" 張牌"+(order>=0?"，已選為"+(mode===3?["現況","阻礙","建議"][order]:"今日提醒"):"，未選取"));
 });
 byId("reveal-button").disabled=selectedSlots.length!==mode;
 announce("selection-status","已選 "+selectedSlots.length+" / "+mode+" 張"+(selectedSlots.length===mode?" · 可以揭牌":""));
}
function addReading(entry){
 if(!capacityAvailable(entry.id))return false;
 const at=state.entries.findIndex(e=>e.id===entry.id);
 if(at>=0)state.entries[at]=entry;else state.entries.unshift(entry);
 state.activeId=entry.id;
 historyPage=0;
 const saved=persist();
 renderReading(entry,true);
 renderHistory();
 announce("save-status",saved?"牌面已記錄，心得輸入後會自動保存。":"牌面保留於本次頁面，重新整理可能遺失；請匯出手札備份。");
 return true;
}
function reveal(){
 if(busy||selectionPhase!=="selecting"||selectedSlots.length!==mode)return;
 flushNote();
 const time=new Date().toISOString();
 const entry={id:entryId(),time,updatedAt:time,mode:String(mode),cardIds:selectedSlots.map(slot=>selectionDeck[slot]),question:byId("question").value.trim()||"此刻，我需要看見什麼？",note:""};
 if(addReading(entry)){
  selectionPhase="revealed";
  byId("choice-panel").hidden=true;
  announce("draw-status","已揭開 "+mode+" 張牌："+entry.cardIds.map(id=>cardMap.get(id).name).join("、")+"。先看圖像，再閱讀提醒。");
 }
}

/* ORACLE_READING_SWITCHER_BEGIN */
const readingNarrow=window.matchMedia("(max-width:899px)");
let readingSelected=0;
function syncReadingPanels(){
 const grid=byId("result-grid"),panels=[...grid.querySelectorAll(".reading-card")],switcher=byId("reading-switcher"),narrow=readingNarrow.matches&&panels.length>1;
 readingSelected=Math.max(0,Math.min(readingSelected,panels.length-1));
 panels.forEach((panel,pos)=>{panel.hidden=narrow&&pos!==readingSelected;});
 if(switcher){switcher.hidden=!narrow||["relations","plan"].includes(readingSection);switcher.querySelectorAll("button").forEach((button,pos)=>{button.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(pos===readingSelected));button.setAttribute("aria-controls",(readingSection==="detail"?"reading-deep-panel-":"reading-panel-")+pos);});}
 syncReadingDepthVisibility();
}
function syncReadingSwitcher(entry,roles){
 readingSelected=0;
 let switcher=byId("reading-switcher");
 if(!switcher){switcher=document.createElement("div");switcher.id="reading-switcher";switcher.className="reading-switcher";switcher.setAttribute("role","group");switcher.setAttribute("aria-label","選擇要閱讀的牌；心得欄會留在同一畫面");byId("result-grid").before(switcher);}
 switcher.replaceChildren();
 if(entry.cardIds.length>1)entry.cardIds.forEach((id,pos)=>{
  const button=document.createElement("button");button.type="button";button.id="reading-choice-"+pos;button.setAttribute("aria-controls","reading-panel-"+pos);
  const role=document.createElement("span");role.textContent=roles[pos];button.append(role,document.createTextNode(cardMap.get(id).name));
  button.addEventListener("click",()=>{readingSelected=pos;syncReadingPanels();});
  switcher.append(button);
 });
 syncReadingPanels();
}
if(readingNarrow.addEventListener)readingNarrow.addEventListener("change",syncReadingPanels);else readingNarrow.addListener(syncReadingPanels);
/* ORACLE_READING_SWITCHER_END */

function renderReading(entry,scroll=false){
 readingSection="overview";readingDetailSection="position";readingEdge=0;readingRelationLens="role";readingPlanStep=0;readingPlanLens="steps";
 const roles=entryRoles(entry),prompts=roleQuestions(entry),single=entry.mode!=="3";
 const grid=byId("result-grid");
 grid.replaceChildren();grid.className=single?"result-grid single":"result-grid";
 entry.cardIds.forEach((id,pos)=>{
  const c=cardMap.get(id),article=document.createElement("article"),headingId="reading-card-"+pos;
  article.className="reading-card"+(single?" single-layout":"");
  article.setAttribute("aria-labelledby",headingId);
  article.style.animationDelay=(pos*70)+"ms";
  article.id="reading-panel-"+pos;
  article.innerHTML='<header class="reading-role"><div class="role">'+roles[pos]+'</div>'+(prompts[pos]?'<p class="role-question">'+prompts[pos]+'</p>':'')+'</header><div class="reading-summary"><div class="reading-art">'+art(c,"reading-"+(++svgCount))+'</div><div class="reading-intro"><h4 id="'+headingId+'">'+c.name+'</h4><p class="keywords">'+c.keywords+'</p><p class="meaning">'+c.message+'</p></div></div><div class="reading-guidance"><div class="action"><span>一個小行動</span><p>'+c.action+'</p></div><div class="action"><span>留給自己的問題</span><p>'+c.prompt+'</p></div></div>';
  grid.append(article);
 });
 syncReadingSwitcher(entry,roles);
 rebuildReadingDepth(entry);
 syncReadingPanels();

 byId("result-title").textContent=single?cardMap.get(entry.cardIds[0]).name+"｜"+readingAnalysis.domainLabel+"的提醒":"現況 → 阻礙 → 建議";
 byId("reading-question").textContent="你的提問："+entry.question;
 let date=byId("reading-date");
 if(!date){date=document.createElement("p");date.id="reading-date";date.className="reading-date";byId("reading-question").parentElement.append(date);}
 date.textContent=taipeiDay(entry.time)+" · "+formatTime(entry.time)+" · "+(entry.mode==="daily"?"每日一牌":entry.mode==="3"?"三張牌":"一張牌");
 byId("journal").value=entry.note;
 byId("result").hidden=false;
 byId("download-status").textContent="";
 if(byId("handout-area"))byId("handout-area").hidden=true;
 if(scroll){byId("result-title").focus({preventScroll:true});byId("result").scrollIntoView({behavior:reduceMotion()?"auto":"smooth",block:"start"});}
/* ORACLE_SOURCE_SUCCESS_HOOK_reading_BEGIN */
if(window.oracleUI&&!byId("result").hidden&&byId("result-grid").querySelector(".reading-card")){
 if(typeof window.oracleUI.syncReadingAfterSuccess==="function")window.oracleUI.syncReadingAfterSuccess({scroll});
 else if(scroll)window.oracleUI.show("reading",{route:false});
}
/* ORACLE_SOURCE_SUCCESS_HOOK_reading_END */
}
function updateDaily(){
 const now=new Date(),day=taipeiDay(now),c=cards[dailyIndex(day)];
 byId("daily-date").textContent=day.slice(5).replace("-"," / ");
 byId("daily-message").textContent=c.name+"｜"+c.message;
 byId("daily-button").dataset.day=day;
}
function openDaily(){
 if(busy)return;
 flushNote();updateDaily();
 byId("choice-panel").hidden=true;selectionPhase="idle";selectedSlots=[];selectionDeck=[];
 const day=taipeiDay(),id="daily-"+day;
 const old=state.entries.find(e=>e.id===id);
 if(old){state.activeId=old.id;persist();renderReading(old,true);announce("save-status",storageReady?"已開啟今天的手札，可繼續記下感受。":"已開啟今天的暫存手札；請下載備份。");return;}
 const time=new Date().toISOString();
 const entry={id,time,updatedAt:time,mode:"daily",cardIds:[cards[dailyIndex(day)].id],question:"今天，我想把注意力放在哪裡？",note:""};
 if(addReading(entry)){
  byId("choice-panel").hidden=true;selectionPhase="idle";selectedSlots=[];
  announce("draw-status","已開啟今日一牌："+cards[dailyIndex(day)].name+"。同一天保留相同牌卡。");
 }
}

function searchGallery(resetPage=true){
 if(resetPage)galleryPage=0;
 const search=byId("card-search").value.trim().normalize("NFKC").toLocaleLowerCase("zh-TW");
 const matches=[];
 document.querySelectorAll("[data-card]").forEach(button=>{
  const c=cards[Number(button.dataset.card)];
  const text=[c.name,c.en,c.keywords,c.message,c.action,c.prompt].join(" ").normalize("NFKC").toLocaleLowerCase("zh-TW");
  const show=(currentFilter==="全部"||c.type===currentFilter)&&(!search||text.includes(search));
  button.hidden=true;if(show)matches.push(button);
 });
 const perPage=8,pageCount=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(matches.length/perPage));
 galleryPage=Math.min(galleryPage,pageCount-1);
 const visible=matches.slice(galleryPage*perPage,(galleryPage+1)*perPage);
 visible.forEach(button=>button.hidden=false);
 announce("gallery-count",matches.length?"顯示 "+(galleryPage*perPage+1)+"–"+(galleryPage*perPage+visible.length)+" / "+matches.length+" 張原創牌 · 點擊瀏覽牌義":"顯示 0 張原創牌");
 byId("empty-gallery").hidden=matches.length!==0;
 let pages=byId("gallery-pages");
 if(!pages){pages=document.createElement("div");pages.id="gallery-pages";pages.className="history-pages";byId("gallery-count").after(pages);}
 pages.replaceChildren();pages.hidden=pageCount<=1;
 if(pageCount>1){
  const prev=document.createElement("button");prev.type="button";prev.className="btn btn-secondary";prev.textContent="上一頁";prev.disabled=galleryPage===0;
  const next=document.createElement("button");next.type="button";next.className="btn btn-secondary";next.textContent="下一頁";next.disabled=galleryPage===pageCount-1;
  const count=document.createElement("span");count.textContent=(galleryPage+1)+" / "+pageCount;
  prev.addEventListener("click",()=>{galleryPage--;searchGallery(false);focusGalleryFirst();});next.addEventListener("click",()=>{galleryPage++;searchGallery(false);focusGalleryFirst();});
  pages.append(prev,count,next);
 }
}
function focusGalleryFirst(){
 const first=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("[data-card]")).find(button=>!button.hidden);
 if(first)first.focus();
}
function resetFilters(){
 currentFilter="全部";byId("card-search").value="";
 document.querySelectorAll("[data-filter]").forEach(button=>button.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(button.dataset.filter==="全部")));
 searchGallery();
}
function openCard(index,trigger){
 const c=cards[index];previousTrigger=trigger;
 byId("modal-art").innerHTML=art(c,"modal-"+(++svgCount));
 for(const [id,text] of [["modal-title",c.name],["modal-keywords",c.keywords],["modal-meaning",c.message],["modal-action",c.action],["modal-prompt",c.prompt]])byId(id).textContent=text;
 byId("card-dialog").showModal();byId("modal-close").focus();
}
function renderHistory(){
 const host=byId("history-list");host.replaceChildren();
 const total=state.entries.length,perPage=6;
 const ordered=[...state.entries].sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(b.time)-Date.parse(a.time));
 const pageCount=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(total/perPage));
 historyPage=Math.min(historyPage,pageCount-1);
 announce("history-count",total?"已留下 "+total+" 份手札":"尚未留下手札");
 byId("empty-notebook").hidden=total>0;
 byId("clear-journal").disabled=!total;
 byId("export-journal").disabled=!total;
 ordered.slice(historyPage*perPage,(historyPage+1)*perPage).forEach(entry=>{
  const article=document.createElement("article");article.className="history-item";
  const meta=document.createElement("div");meta.className="history-meta";
  const date=document.createElement("time");date.dateTime=entry.time;date.textContent=taipeiDay(entry.time)+" · "+formatTime(entry.time);
  const modeLabel=document.createElement("span");modeLabel.textContent=entry.mode==="daily"?"每日一牌":entry.mode==="3"?"三張牌":"一張牌";
  meta.append(date,modeLabel);
  const title=document.createElement("h3");title.textContent=entry.cardIds.map(id=>cardMap.get(id).name).join(" · ");
  const question=document.createElement("p");question.className="history-question";question.textContent=entry.question;
  const note=document.createElement("p");note.className="history-note";note.textContent=entry.note||"還留著空白，隨時可以補上感受。";
  const actions=document.createElement("div");actions.className="history-buttons";
  const open=document.createElement("button");open.type="button";open.textContent="開啟手札";open.setAttribute("aria-label","開啟 "+title.textContent+" 手札");
  open.addEventListener("click",()=>{flushNote();byId("choice-panel").hidden=true;selectionPhase="idle";selectedSlots=[];selectionDeck=[];state.activeId=entry.id;persist();renderReading(entry,true);announce("save-status",storageReady?"已開啟這份手札，修改後會自動保存。":"已開啟本次暫存手札；請下載備份。");});
  const del=document.createElement("button");del.type="button";del.className="delete-entry";del.textContent="刪除";del.setAttribute("aria-label","刪除 "+title.textContent+" 手札");
  del.addEventListener("click",()=>openDelete(entry.id,del));
  actions.append(open,del);article.append(meta,title,question,note,actions);host.append(article);
 });
 let pages=byId("history-pages");
 if(!pages){pages=document.createElement("div");pages.id="history-pages";pages.className="history-pages";host.after(pages);}
 pages.replaceChildren();pages.hidden=pageCount<=1;
 if(pageCount>1){
  const prev=document.createElement("button");prev.type="button";prev.className="btn btn-secondary";prev.textContent="上一頁";prev.disabled=historyPage===0;
  const next=document.createElement("button");next.type="button";next.className="btn btn-secondary";next.textContent="下一頁";next.disabled=historyPage===pageCount-1;
  const count=document.createElement("span");count.textContent=(historyPage+1)+" / "+pageCount;
  prev.addEventListener("click",()=>{historyPage--;renderHistory();byId("notebook-title").setAttribute("tabindex","-1");byId("notebook-title").focus({preventScroll:true});});next.addEventListener("click",()=>{historyPage++;renderHistory();byId("notebook-title").setAttribute("tabindex","-1");byId("notebook-title").focus({preventScroll:true});});
  pages.append(prev,count,next);
 }
}
function openDelete(id,trigger){
 flushNote();pendingDelete=id;deleteTrigger=trigger;
 byId("delete-title").textContent=id==="all"?"清空全部手札？":"刪除這份手札？";
 byId("delete-description").textContent=id==="all"?"將刪除目前的 "+state.entries.length+" 份手札。你可以先匯出備份。":"刪除後，可透過先前的匯出檔案重新匯入。";
 byId("delete-dialog").returnValue="";
 byId("delete-dialog").showModal();
}
function finishDelete(){
 if(byId("delete-dialog").returnValue!=="delete"){if(deleteTrigger)deleteTrigger.focus();pendingDelete=null;return;}
 if(pendingDelete==="all"){state.entries=[];state.activeId=null;}
 else {state.entries=state.entries.filter(e=>e.id!==pendingDelete);if(state.activeId===pendingDelete)state.activeId=null;}
 if(!state.activeId){byId("result").hidden=true;byId("journal").value="";}
 persist();historyPage=0;renderHistory();
 announce("history-status",pendingDelete==="all"?"已清空目前的手札紀錄。":"已刪除這份手札。");
 pendingDelete=null;byId("notebook-title").setAttribute("tabindex","-1");byId("notebook-title").focus({preventScroll:true});
}
function downloadFile(text,name,type){
 const blob=new Blob([text],{type}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),link=document.createElement("a");
 link.href=url;link.download=name;document.body.append(link);link.click();link.remove();
 window.setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
}
function showHandout(text,message){
 let area=byId("handout-area");
 if(!area){
  area=document.createElement("div");area.id="handout-area";area.className="handout-area";
  const label=document.createElement("label");label.htmlFor="handout-text";label.textContent="手札文字（可選取複製）";
  const box=document.createElement("textarea");box.id="handout-text";box.readOnly=true;box.rows=8;
  const close=document.createElement("button");close.type="button";close.id="close-handout";close.className="btn btn-outline";close.textContent="收起文字";
  close.addEventListener("click",()=>{area.hidden=true;announce("download-status","");byId("download").focus({preventScroll:true});});
  area.append(label,box,close);byId("download-status").parentElement.append(area);
 }
 area.hidden=false;byId("handout-text").value=text;announce("download-status",message);
}
async function copyReading(){
 saveNote(false);const e=currentEntry();if(!e)return;
 const text=readingText(e);
 try{
  if(!navigator.clipboard||!navigator.clipboard.writeText)throw new Error("Clipboard unavailable");
  await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
  announce("download-status","已複製這次提醒與手札。");
 }catch(error){showHandout(text,"此環境無法自動複製，請選取下方文字留存。");byId("handout-text").focus();byId("handout-text").select();}
}
function downloadReading(){
 saveNote(false);const e=currentEntry();if(!e)return;
 const text=readingText(e);
 try{downloadFile("\uFEFF"+text,"月光手札-"+taipeiDay(e.time)+".txt","text/plain;charset=utf-8");showHandout(text,"已建立手札下載；也可從下方文字複製留存。");}
 catch(error){showHandout(text,"此環境限制下載，請複製下方手札文字。");}
}
function exportJournal(){
 flushNote();if(!state.entries.length)return;
 const json=JSON.stringify(state,null,2);
 byId("backup-text-area").hidden=false;byId("backup-text").value=json;
 try{downloadFile(json,"月光手札備份-"+taipeiDay()+".json","application/json");announce("history-status","已建立備份下載。也可複製下方 JSON 文字，保存為 .json 檔案。");}
 catch(error){announce("history-status","此環境限制下載，請複製下方 JSON 文字，保存為 .json 檔案。");}
}
function importJournal(file){
 if(!file)return;
 if(file.size>5000000){announce("history-status","檔案過大，請選擇小於 5 MB 的手札 JSON 備份。");return;}
 flushNote();
 const reader=new FileReader();
 reader.onerror=()=>announce("history-status","無法讀取檔案，請重新選擇手札 JSON 備份。");
 reader.onload=()=>{
  const parsed=parseState(String(reader.result));
  if(parsed.status!=="ok"){announce("history-status","這份檔案不是有效的月光手札備份，現有紀錄已保留。");return;}
  const merged=mergeEntries(state.entries,parsed.state.entries);
  if(merged.length>MAX_ENTRIES){announce("history-status","合併後超過 "+MAX_ENTRIES+" 份。請先匯出並整理現有紀錄，這次未匯入。");return;}
  state.entries=merged;
  const saved=persist();historyPage=0;renderHistory();
  const active=currentEntry();if(active)renderReading(active,false);
  announce("history-status","已合併 "+parsed.state.entries.length+" 份手札，共 "+merged.length+" 份。"+(saved?"已儲存於瀏覽器。":"目前為本次暫存，請匯出備份。"));
 };
 reader.readAsText(file);
}

 try{downloadFile(pristineSite,"神諭之間.html","text/html;charset=utf-8");}catch(error){}
 byId("source-text").value=pristineSite;byId("source-dialog").showModal();
}

function initializeStorage(){
 return new Promise(resolve=>{
  setBusy(true);
  byId("draw-button").textContent="準備你的月光手札……";
  byId("import-journal").disabled=true;
  let fallback=null;
  const finish=()=>{
   if(storageInitDone)return;
   storageInitDone=true;
   if(fallback!==null)window.clearTimeout(fallback);
   announce("storage-note",storageMessage());
   renderHistory();updateDaily();searchGallery();
   const restored=currentEntry();
   if(restored){renderReading(restored,false);announce("save-status",storageReady?"已恢復上次的手札，可以繼續記下感受。":"已開啟可讀取的手札，本次修改請匯出備份。");}
   setBusy(false);byId("import-journal").disabled=false;
   resolve();
  };
  if(!navigator.locks||typeof navigator.locks.request!=="function"){
   storageReason="unsupported";loadStorage(false);finish();return;
  }
  fallback=window.setTimeout(()=>{if(storageInitDone)return;storageReason="unavailable";loadStorage(false);finish();},2000);
  try{
   navigator.locks.request(STORAGE_KEY+":writer",{mode:"exclusive",ifAvailable:true},async lock=>{
    if(storageInitDone)return;
    if(!lock){storageReason="other-tab";loadStorage(false);finish();return;}
    const held=new Promise(release=>{releaseStorageLock=release;});
    loadStorage(true);finish();
    if(storageReady)await held;
   }).catch(()=>{if(storageInitDone)return;storageReason="unavailable";loadStorage(false);finish();});
  }catch(error){storageReason="unavailable";loadStorage(false);finish();}
 });
}

const storageInitialized=initializeStorage();

Things》，把它定位為辨認、觀看與陪伴情緒的工具，並提供日記、冥想或對話等使用方向。這種主張把注意力放在讀者如何理解當下：圖像是否幫助你找到一個感受的名稱、整理矛盾，或想起被忽略的需求。這些是能被描述的使用經驗，不需要先假設卡片知道尚未發生的事，也不必把每一次抽牌都轉成事件判決。"},{"heading":"預測需要事先說清楚條件","text":"如果有人說一張牌能預告某件事，至少需要界定事件是什麼、何時發生、如何判斷命中，並記錄沒有命中的情況。否則，事後重新解釋文字，可能讓幾乎任何結果都看似吻合。這裡提出的是檢查預測主張的方法，不是為所有神諭卡設定統一用途。作品的作者可以採靈性語言，讀者也可以持有信念；公開談準確率時，仍應提供與該主張相稱的資料。"},{"heading":"共鳴值得記錄但不等於驗證","text":"Forer的經典研究摘要提醒，讀者接受一段描述，不等於描述因此已被證實正確；那項研究討論的是人格描述，並非神諭卡的專屬試驗。借用這個提醒，可以在手札中分別寫下『它讓我感到什麼』及『我觀察到什麼』。前者保留個人的意義，後者讓實際情況有自己的位置。當牌組提供一個可行的小提醒，就先檢視它如何幫助思考，不必把所有共鳴都升格為預知能力的證據。"}],"tags":["反思","預測","證據"],"sourceIds":["F11","F10"],"readingType":"研究整理","relatedTopicIds":["oracle-133"]},{"id":"oracle-12","unit":12,"kind":"topic","title":"信念、文化與證據各有位置","category":"認識與歷史","summary":"神諭卡中的天使、指導靈與能量等語言，可能屬於作者信念、文化象徵或作品設定。尊重這些語言，與確認外在主張是否有證據，可以同時進行。清楚標示來源與層次，讓不同背景的讀者都能理解作品而保有自己的判斷。","sections":[{"heading":"先辨認作者正在說哪種話","text":"《Ask Cards》以高我、天使及指導靈提供支持作為作品世界觀。整理這類資料時，應寫『作者將牌組定位為……』，而不是直接宣布所有讀者都有相同的靈性經驗。神話、宗教與個人靈性並不必然使用同一套概念；即使文字相似，含義也可能不同。先理解來源的語境，比把所有精神性詞語都合併成一個普遍系統更能尊重作者與讀者。"},{"heading":"文化意義不是效果測量","text":"某個象徵在一個社群中很重要，可以說明它如何形成歸屬、故事或價值觀，但不能直接證明牌組能預測外在事件。個人感到安定，也不等於作品在所有人身上都會產生相同效果。相反地，科學研究沒有測量某個精神經驗，也不代表研究能替每個人決定其全部生命意義。知識庫可以把文化介紹、使用者敘述與效果主張分層呈現，避免任何一層冒充另外一層的答案。"},{"heading":"閱讀時保留判斷空間","text":"面對一副牌，可以先問：它使用哪些信念語言？我是否願意沿用？哪些部分只當成象徵，哪些部分需要實際資訊才能判斷？Forer的研究摘要支持慎重看待『覺得描述很準』與客觀有效性之間的差距，但它不是神諭卡信仰的全面裁決。本站的整理方式是讓來源可追查，並把信念、方法與研究結果明確區分；讀者不必先接受某種神意說法，才有資格欣賞圖像或進行反思。"}],"tags":["信念","文化","來源分層"],"sourceIds":["F03","F10"],"readingType":"研究整理","relatedTopicIds":[]},{"id":"oracle-13","unit":13,"kind":"topic","title":"原創牌組與《月光手札》的定位","category":"認識與歷史","summary":"原創神諭卡可以由創作者自行建立主題、牌名、圖像與閱讀方法，不必沿用另一副牌的牌義。《神諭之間》的24張《月光手札》以日常感受、自我對話與行動提醒為核心。它是本站獨立作品，閱讀時應依本站說明理解。","sections":[{"heading":"原創指的是作品自己的設計","text":"原創不只是把既有牌義換成新的畫面，也包括清楚建立作品想談的主題、訊息如何組織，以及讀者如何使用它。Christina Mrozik以情緒、生態與身體圖像設計50張牌，就是一個公開的當代創作例子。不同作者可以有不同目的，作品無須假稱來自遠古或某種普遍權威才有價值。對讀者而言，知道牌組是誰創作、想做什麼，比看到一個模糊的神秘傳承宣稱更有助於理解。"},{"heading":"本站24張牌如何使用","text":"《月光手札》是本站為日常反思建立的24張牌，配合每日一牌、單張及三張閱讀。牌面訊息、行動提醒和書寫問題，服務於整理此刻的感受與可以採取的下一步。三張閱讀採現況、阻礙及建議的順序；這是本站設計的閱讀框架，不是全部神諭卡都必須遵守的牌陣。閱讀結果應結合你自己的生活經驗，並以手札保存當時的理解，而不是把文字視為事件已被判定。"},{"heading":"與其他作品並讀時","text":"讀者可以把《月光手札》和其他牌組並讀，但應保留各自的作者與規則。若兩副牌都談休息，訊息相似不表示它們的整套牌義可以互換；若兩張牌出現不同提醒，也可以回到問題情境，看它們是否各自注意了不同層面。本站知識庫介紹其他作品時，只整理可查證的出版資料與公開方法，不把其完整手冊或牌義複製成本站內容。欣賞不同創作，也包括讓每副牌保持自己的聲音。"}],"tags":["原創","月光手札","本站方法"],"sourceIds":["F14","F11"],"readingType":"作者方法","relatedTopicIds":["oracle-133"]},{"id":"oracle-14","unit":14,"kind":"topic","title":"獨立製作、群眾募資與出版社牌組","category":"認識與歷史","summary":"神諭卡可以由藝術家直接製作與販售，也可以由出版社編輯、翻譯和發行。這些路徑影響版本資訊、配送及配套文件，卻不能單憑出版形式判斷作品更準或更有價值。選牌時先核對作者、規格、供貨狀態及官方說明。","sections":[{"heading":"藝術家直接製作的例子","text":"Christina 的公開提問指南建議避開只能回答是或否的問題，改問需要知道什麼，以及可以採取哪些步驟。這是她的教學方法，並非所有神諭牌共同遵守的規則。把問題打開，主要改變的是讀牌時的注意力：你會更容易思考原因、處境和選擇，而不只是把一句牌文判成肯定或否定。"},{"heading":"本站示例：把焦點放回自己","text":"「他愛不愛我」可改為「這段關係中，我需要理解自己的哪些需求？」；「我會不會成功」可改為「這個計畫目前值得留意的阻力是什麼？」；「我該不該離職」可改為「做決定前，我還需要蒐集哪些資訊？」這些是本站設計的反思問句。它們不能代替對話、工作資料或實際評估，卻能幫助整理下一步要查證的事情。"},{"heading":"保留不確定，讓答案能被修正","text":"抽牌前寫下問句與原先想法，抽牌後再寫一個可能解讀和一個不同解讀。若某張牌沒有明顯關聯，記錄「暫時沒有連結」即可，不必勉強湊出答案。OpenStax Campbell Boland 等作者提供過日抽與記錄的方法，但每副牌的規則不同。你不必天天抽，也不必把抽到的牌當作當日必然事件；重點是形成可回顧的閱讀紀錄。","sections":[{"heading":"一張牌，留下一個注意點","text":"Yasmin Boland 在公開文章中介紹早晨抽一張牌並寫日誌的做法；Colette Baron-Reid 的日誌指南也提供每日選牌、看圖和閱讀手冊的流程。兩位作者用各自的靈性語言描述這項習慣。本站把它整理成反思練習：問「今天我希望留意什麼」，抽牌後選一個值得帶進日常的提醒，不必將整天所有事情都解釋成這張牌的預告。"},{"heading":"本站原創：早晚各留一句","text":"早上寫下最先注意到的圖像、今天的感受和一個小提醒；晚上再補一句實際發生的事情。有連結的部分可以記錄，沒有連結的部分也保留，不用只收藏看似命中的例子。當生活忙碌，可以只讀一個關鍵字，也可以跳過一天。持續閱讀的目的在於熟悉牌組與自己，並沒有必須連續完成多少天才有效的共同標準。"},{"heading":"使用本站每日一牌","text":"《神諭之間》的每日一牌依臺北日期固定同一天的牌，適合把同一個提示帶回來補寫感受。它和每次重新洗牌、隨機抽一張的做法不同。每日牌不代表所有使用者一定有同樣經歷，也不因為日期固定而具備預測能力。若你偏好當下重新選牌，可使用一張牌模式，並在手札記下選用的方式，避免把不同流程混成一次閱讀。"}],"tags":["每日一牌","日誌","習慣"],"sourceIds":["P06","P02","F14"],"readingType":"作者方法","relatedTopicIds":["oracle-114"]},{"id":"oracle-24","unit":24,"kind":"topic","title":"三張牌：現況、阻礙、建議","category":"抽牌與閱讀","summary":"三張牌可以讓閱讀有三個清楚的位置。本網站採用「現況／阻礙／建議」，這是現代反思用的示例，並不是所有神諭牌共有的古老牌陣。先寫好每個位置的問題，再依序讀圖與手冊；最後將建議收斂成一個你願意嘗試的小步驟。","sections":[{"heading":"先定義位置，再翻開牌","text":"Rebecca Campbell
